Keep\nthe scalar body and add a kernel in front of it:\n\n```cpp\n#include \"../dsp/simd.hpp\"\n\nnamespace kernels {\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\nCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void mul_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept {\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i += 8)\n        _mm256_store_ps(out + i, _mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_load_ps(a + i), _mm256_load_ps(b + i)));\n}\n#endif\n}\n\ninline void op_mul(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    // ... resolve a, b, out ...\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n    if (simd::has(simd::Isa::Avx2)) { kernels::mul_avx2(a, b, out); return; }\n#endif\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) out[i] = a[i] * b[i];  // fallback stays\n}\n```\n\n- `CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2` is `[[gnu::target(\"avx2\")]]`; no compiler flags change.\n  FMA is **not** enabled (it would change rounding).\n- Buffers from `ctx.buffers->get()` are 32-byte aligned; `BLOCK_SIZE` is 128.\n- You may also add NEON (`CEDAR_SIMD_NEON`) or WASM SIMD128\n  (`CEDAR_SIMD_WASM`) paths; they are measured later on other machines, not\n  gated here.\n\n## Rules (enforced by the verifier \u2014 violations are rejected automatically)\n\n1. You may only edit: `cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/**`, `cedar/include/cedar/dsp/simd*.hpp`, `cedar/src/dsp/simd*.cpp`. Any other edit (benchmarks, tests,\n   experiments, CMake, scripts) rejects the whole attempt.\n2. **Never delete or change the scalar body.** It is the oracle and the\n   fallback; the verifier runs it with SIMD disabled and requires\n   bit-identical output to the original.\n3. Output must be **bit-identical** to the original for every opcode.\n   No approximations are allowed for these opcodes.\n4. The final verification also runs every opcode on **randomised stimuli you\n   cannot see** (different signals, levels down to denormals, parameter\n   trajectories). A kernel that only works for the stimuli in the benchmark\n   source fails there.\n5. No allocation, locks or syscalls in a kernel.  // draws per-stimulus variations in hidden mode\n    59\t\n    60\tfloat frac(std::size_t n, std::size_t period) {\n    61\t    return static_cast<float>(n % period) / static_cast<float>(period);\n    62\t}\n    63\t\n    64\t// Audio-rate signal input: seeded noise \u2192 log sine sweep \u2192 impulse train \u2192\n    65\t// ramp, a quarter of the stimulus window each (PRD \u00a74.3 fixed stimuli).\n    66\tstruct AudioStim {\n    67\t    float gain;\n    68\t    std::vector<float> noise;\n    69\t    explicit AudioStim(float g) : gain(g), noise(STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE) {\n    70\t        std::mt19937 rng(g_seed ? g_seed : 0xCEDA5u);\n    71\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> d(-1.0f, 1.0f);\n    72\t        for (auto& x : noise) x = d(rng);\n    73\t        if (g_seed) make_hidden(rng);\n    74\t    }\n    75\t    // Hidden mode: 8 segments, each a random choice of noise at a random\n    76\t    // gain (from near-denormal to 4x the nominal level), sparse random\n    77\t    // impulses, DC steps, or silence. Every seed is guaranteed one\n    78\t    // near-denormal noise segment (1e-35..1e-25) and one hot one (2-4x),\n    79\t    // at random positions, so that coverage never depends on luck.\n    80\t    void make_hidden(std::mt19937& rng) {\n    81\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> u(0.0f, 1.0f);\n    82\t        constexpr std::size_t seg = STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE / 8;\n    83\t        const auto tiny_seg = static_cast<std::size_t>(u(rng) * 8.0f) % 8;\n    84\t        const auto hot_seg = (tiny_seg + 1 + static_cast<std::size_t>(u(rng) * 7.0f) % 7) % 8;\n    85\t        for (std::size_t s = 0; s < 8; ++s) {\n    86\t            int kind = static_cast<int>(u(rng) * 4.0f);\n    87\t            float level = gain * std::pow(10.0f, -30.0f * u(rng) * u(rng)) * (1.0f + 3.0f * u(rng));\n    88\t            if (s == tiny_seg) { kind = 0; level = gain * std::pow(10.0f, -25.0f - 10.0f * u(rng)); }\n    89\t            if (s == hot_seg) { kind = 0; level = gain * (2.0f + 2.0f * u(rng)); }\n    90\t            float dc = level * (2.0f * u(rng) - 1.0f);\n    91\t            for (std::size_t k = s * seg; k < (s + 1) * seg; ++k) {\n    92\t                switch (kind) {\n    93\t                    case 0: noise[k] *= level; break;\n    94\t                    case 1: noise[k] = u(rng) < 0.002f ? level * (u(rng) < 0.5f ? -1.0f : 1.0f) : 0.0f; break;\n    95\t                    case 2: noise[k] = (k % 4096 == 0) ? (dc = level * (2.0f * u(rng) - 1.0f)) : dc; break;\n    96\t                    default: noise[k] = 0.0f; break;\n    97\t                }\n    98\t            }\n    99\t        }\n   100\t    }"

The run is `unstable` if the governor is not `performance` or the fresh\n    11\tbaseline drifts > 10 % from the pinned one in baselines.json.\n    12\t\"\"\"\n    13\timport argparse\n    14\timport json\n    15\timport os\n    16\timport statistics\n    17\timport subprocess\n    18\timport sys\n    19\tfrom pathlib import Path\n    20\t\n    21\tHERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n    22\tPINNED = HERE / \"baselines.json\"\n    23\tCPU = os.environ.get(\"AUTORESEARCH_CPU\", \"2\")  # a P-core on the i7-12700KF\n    24\tDRIFT_LIMIT = 0.10\n    25\tMIN_SPEEDUP = 1.05\n    26\t\n    27\t\n    28\tdef run(binary, opcode, reps):\n    29\t    # A list is a full command prefix (node / ssh legs); a path is a local\n    30\t    # native binary, pinned to one P-core.\n    31\t    prefix = list(binary) if isinstance(binary, (list, tuple)) else [\"taskset\", \"-c\", CPU, str(binary)]\n    32\t    cmd = [*prefix, \"--opcode\", opcode, \"--reps\", str(reps), \"--json\"]\n    33\t    out = subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout\n    34\t    return json.loads(out)\n    35\t\n    36\t\n    37\tdef measure(opcode, baseline, candidate=None, rounds=5, reps=500, check_pinned=True):\n    38\t    base, cand = [], []\n    39\t    for _ in range(rounds):\n    40\t        base.append(run(baseline, opcode, reps))\n    41\t        if candidate:\n    42\t            cand.append(run(candidate, opcode, reps))\n    43\t    bmeds = [r[\"ns_per_block_median\"] for r in base]\n    44\t    bmed = statistics.median(bmeds)\n    45\t    # Noise band: run-to-run spread of the baseline median against itself.\n    46\t    # A speedup inside it is not a speedup. r[\"ns_p10\"]) / r[\"ns_per_block_median\"] for r in base)\n    50\t    res = {\"opcode\": opcode, \"baseline_ns\": round(bmed, 2), \"noise_band\": round(band, 4),\n    51\t           \"p10_p90_spread\": round(spread, 4),\n    52\t           \"isa_baseline\": base[0][\"isa\"], \"governor\": base[0][\"governor\"], \"cpu\": base[0][\"cpu\"]}\n    53\t    unstable = []\n    54\t    if res[\"governor\"] != \"performance\":\n    55\t        unstable.append(f\"governor={res['governor']}\")\n    56\t    pinned = json.loads(PINNED.read_text()).get(\"opcodes\", {}) \\\n    57\t        if check_pinned and PINNED.exists() else {}  # pins are x86 gate-machine numbers\n    58\t    if opcode in pinned:\n    59\t        drift = abs(bmed - pinned[opcode]) / pinned[opcode]\n    60\t        res[\"baseline_drift\"] = round(drift, 4)\n    61\t        if drift > DRIFT_LIMIT:\n    62\t            unstable.append(f\"baseline drift {drift:.1%} vs pinned\")\n    63\t    if candidate:\n    64\t        cmed = statistics.median(r[\"ns_per_block_median\"] for r in cand)\n    65\t        speedup = bmed / cmed\n    66\t        res.update(candidate_ns=round(cmed, 2), speedup=round(speedup, 3),\n    67\t                   isa_candidate=cand[0][\"isa\"],\n    68\t                   faster=speedup >= MIN_SPEEDUP and (1 - cmed / bmed) > band)\n    69\t    res[\"unstable\"] = unstable or False\n    70\t    return res\n    71\t\n    72\t\n    73\tdef main():\n    74\t    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    75\t    ap.add_argument(\"--opcode\")\n    76\t    ap.add_argument(\"--baseline\", default=\"build/release/bin/cedar_bench\")\n    77\t    ap.add_argument(\"--candidate\")\n    78\t    ap.add_argument(\"--rounds\", type=int, default=5)\n    79\t    ap.add_argument(\"--reps\", type=int, default=500)\n    80\t    ap.add_argument(\"--pin\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"re-record baselines.json for every opcode\")\n    81\t    a = ap.parse_args()\n    82\t\n    83\t    if a.pin:\n    84\t        names = subprocess.run([a.baseline, \"--list\"], check=True, capture_output=True,\n    85\t                               text=True).stdout.split()\n    86\t        rows = {n: measure(n, a.baseline, rounds=a.rounds, reps=a.reps) for n in names}\n    87\t        first = next(iter(rows.values()))\n    88\t        PINNED.write_text(json.dumps({\n    89\t            \"cpu\": first[\"cpu\"], \"governor\": first[\"governor\"], \"pinned_cpu\": CPU,\n    90\t            \"opcodes\": {n: r[\"baseline_ns\"] for n, r in rows.items()},\n    91\t            \"noise_band\": {n: r[\"noise_band\"] for n, r in rows.items()},\n    92\t        }, indent=2) + \"\\n\")\n    93\t        for n, r in sorted(rows.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1][\"baseline_ns\"]):\n    94\t            print(f\"{n:22s} {r['baseline_ns']:10.2f} ns/block  band {r['noise_band']:.1%}\")\n    95\t        return\n    96\t    if not a.opcode:\n    97\t        ap.error(\"--opcode required\")\n    98\t    print(json.dumps(measure(a.opcode, a.baseline, a.candidate, a.rounds, a.reps)))\n    99\t\n   100\t\n   101\tif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n   102\t    sys.exit(main())"

Commit K (followup, `d95aa8b`): `VM::execute_step` unifies subprogram-body dispatch so BLOCK_CALL / BLOCK_BIND / SKIP_IF / LOOP_STATIC dispatch from inside every body context, not just `execute_program`'s main loop.\ndocs/prd-runtime-functions-control-flow.md:4: > - **L2 (Phase 2) \u2014 SHIPPED.** Shipped first: the `#inline` annotation (lexer `#` token + statement-position parsing) and recursion rejection (E240/E244/E246/E249). Shipped completing L2: eligible non-`#inline` user `fn`s called from \u22652 sites compile **once** into a subprogram block and dispatch at runtime via a real `BLOCK_CALL` opcode \u2014 body stored once in `ProgramSlot::instructions[]`, relieving program-size / i-cache pressure. **Design pivot from \u00a74.2 \u2014 the swap-time `expand_block_calls()` model was abandoned.** Compile-time expansion produces audio bytecode byte-identical to per-site inlining (zero size/i-cache benefit \u2014 see \u00a76.1); instead L2 reuses L3's already-shipped runtime subprogram table (`ProgramSlot::blocks[]`, the block-aware load handshake, `set_state_id_xor` isolation). `BLOCK_CALL` carries `block_id` in `inst.rate`, caller args in `inst.inputs[0..4]`, result in `inst.out_buffer`, a per-call-site disambiguator in `inst.state_id`; `BlockEntry` gained `param_base` + `body_output` (12 bytes). `RET` stays a reserved no-op \u2014 body length lives in `BlockEntry::length`. Eligibility = speculative-compile-with-rollback (cheap signature gate, then a one-shot body compile rolled back on any error / empty-or-oversize body / nested dispatch opcode); ineligible or non-dispatchable call sites (string/array/record/`_` args) fall back to inlining \u2014 a pure optimization. Per-call-site state isolation + hot-swap continuity via a `block:<fn>@callsite_<N>` path hash XOR-composed by the VM. Rollback knob: the `inline_all_fns` compiler option forces the pre-L2 inline-everywhere behavior. **`BLOCK_BIND` (\u00a74.2) shipped as a follow-up (2026-05-20):** the >5-param slot path is now built \u2014 `fn`s with 6..`MAX_SHARED_FN_PARAMS` (32) parameters are shareable. Params 0..4 ride `BLOCK_CALL.inputs[0..4]`; params 5..N-1 ride a contiguous run of `BLOCK_BIND` opcodes (slot index in `rate`, source buffer in `inputs[0]`) emitted immediately before the `BLOCK_CALL`. The VM dispatch loop scans the run and copies the extra slots into the body's convention param bank. `fn`s beyond 32 params still fall back to inlining (graceful, never an error). **Dispatch-uniformity followup (2026-05-24, `d95aa8b`):** the L2 ship wired `BLOCK_CALL` / `BLOCK_BIND` / `SKIP_IF_*` / `LOOP_STATIC` into `execute_program`'s main IP loop but left every subprogram-body runner (`run_voice_pool`, `run_foreach_per_iteration`, `run_foreach_shared`, `run_event_map_closure`, `run_event_filter_closure`, `execute_block_call`'s own body loop) driving instructions through `VM::execute()` directly \u2014 whose switch has no case for the meta-opcodes, so any `BLOCK_CALL` inside a subprogram body silently no-op'd. A new `VM::execute_step(slot, body, ip) -> next_ip` helper owns the meta-opcode dispatch and is now the single entry point for both the main loop and every body runner; nested `BLOCK_CALL` (fn-in-fn, fn-in-event-map-closure, fn-in-voice-body) now dispatches correctly."

Handled here, not via execute().\n   497\t        const float* predicate = ctx_.buffers->get(inst.inputs[0]);\n   498\t        return skip_if_next_ip(inst, predicate[0], ip);\n   499\t      }\n   500\t      case Opcode::LOOP_STATIC: {\n   501\t        // Static loop: re-run the next body_len instructions `count` times.\n   502\t        // body_len=rate, count=out_buffer. State is shared across passes.\n   503\t        // Inner dispatch goes back through execute_step so nested meta-ops\n   504\t        // (BLOCK_CALL etc.) inside the LOOP_STATIC body work too.\n   505\t        const std::size_t body_len = inst.rate;\n   506\t        const std::uint16_t iterations = inst.out_buffer;\n   507\t        for (std::uint16_t it = 0; it < iterations; ++it) {\n   508\t            std::size_t inner = ip + 1;\n   509\t            const std::size_t inner_end = ip + 1 + body_len;\n   510\t            while (inner < inner_end) {\n   511\t                inner = execute_step(slot, body, inner);\n   512\t            }\n   513\t        }\n   514\t        return ip + body_len + 1;\n   515\t      }\n   516\t      case Opcode::BLOCK_CALL:\n   517\t        // L2: dispatch a shared user-`fn` body from the subprogram table.\n   518\t        // The body is not inline \u2014 advance by one. `slot` must be non-null\n   519\t        // for BLOCK_CALL to resolve; nullptr means the body came from a\n   520\t        // legacy non-slot codepath and codegen guarantees no BLOCK_CALL.\n   521\t        if (slot != nullptr) {\n   522\t            execute_block_call(slot, body, ip);\n   523\t        }\n   524\t        return ip + 1;\n   525\t      case Opcode::BLOCK_BIND: {\n   526\t        // L2 >5-param call: a contiguous run of BLOCK_BIND carries the\n   527\t        // logical param slots >=5 for the BLOCK_CALL that terminates the\n   528\t        // run. Scan the run, then dispatch the BLOCK_CALL with the binds.\n   529\t        const std::size_t bind_start = ip;\n   530\t        const std::size_t end = body.size();\n   531\t        std::size_t scan = ip;\n   532\t        while (scan < end && body[scan].opcode == Opcode::BLOCK_BIND) {\n   533\t            ++scan;\n   534\t        }\n   535\t        assert(scan < end && body[scan].opcode == Opcode::BLOCK_CALL &&\n   536\t               \"BLOCK_BIND run not terminated by BLOCK_CALL\");\n   537\t        if (slot != nullptr && scan < end &&\n   538\t            body[scan].opcode == Opcode::BLOCK_CALL) {\n   539\t            execute_block_call(slot, body, scan,\n   540\t                               body.subspan(bind_start, scan - bind_start));\n   541\t        }\n   542\t        return scan + 1;\n   543\t      }\n   544\t      case Opcode::RET:\n   545\t        // RET terminates a subprogram body. Body runners bound themselves\n   546\t        // by body.size(), so reaching the final instruction naturally is\n   547\t        // the normal exit \u2014 a mid-body RET means codegen emitted stray\n   548\t        // control flow. A speedup inside that band is not a speedup.\n   253\t- Records CPU model and scaling governor; refuses to gate (warns and marks the\n   254\t  run `unstable`) if the governor is not `performance`.\n   255\t\n   256\t### 5.4 Log schema\n   257\t\n   258\tPer iteration, appended to `runs/<run-id>/iterations.jsonl`:\n   259\t\n   260\t```json\n   261\t{\"run_id\":\"2026-09-28T14:03Z_opus5_op_distort_tanh\",\"iteration\":3,\n   262\t \"model\":\"claude-opus-5\",\"provider\":\"claude-code\",\n   263\t \"opcode\":\"op_distort_tanh\",\"isa_target\":\"avx2\",\n   264\t \"tokens\":{\"input\":18422,\"output\":2811,\"cached\":12000},\n   265\t \"cost_usd\":0.41,\"wall_clock_s\":96.3,\"gpu_s\":null,\n   266\t \"verdict\":\"accept\",\"reject_reason\":null,\n   267\t \"gates\":{\"build\":\"pass\",\"equality\":\"approx\",\"null_test_dbfs\":-112.4,\n   268\t          \"unit\":\"pass\",\"zero_alloc\":\"pass\",\"experiment\":\"pass\"},\n   269\t \"bench\":{\"baseline_ns\":118.7,\"candidate_ns\":41.9,\"speedup\":2.83,\"noise_band\":0.031},\n   270\t \"patch_sha\":\"...\",\"branch\":\"autoresearch/opus5-distort-tanh\"}\n   271\t```\n   272\t\n   273\tPer run, `runs/<run-id>/summary.json`: totals (tokens, USD, wall clock, GPU\n   274\tseconds), best speedup, iteration index of first accept, accept/reject counts.\n   275\t\n   276\t### 5.5 Anti-cheat\n   277\t\n   278\tThe loop measures the thing it optimises, so the model must not be able to touch\n   279\tthe measurement. `verify.sh` enforces a **write allowlist**:\n   280\t\n   281\t- Writable: `cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/**`, `cedar/include/cedar/dsp/simd*.hpp`,\n   282\t  `cedar/src/dsp/simd*.cpp`.\n   283\t- Everything else \u2014 benchmarks, tests, experiments, budgets, CMake \u2014 is restored\n   284\t  from a pristine checkout before every verify. A patch touching them is an\n   285\t  automatic reject, logged as `reject_reason: \"out-of-allowlist edit\"`.\n   286\t\n   287\tThis is also a talk beat: the first thing you defend against is the optimiser\n   288\toptimising the scoreboard.\n   289\t\n   290\t---\n   291\t\n   292\t## 6. Edge Cases\n   293\t\n   294\t1. **Benchmark noise / thermal drift.** Long runs heat the machine and slow late\n   295\t   iterations. Mitigation: interleave baseline re-measurement every iteration\n   296\t   (speedup is always ratio-to-a-fresh-baseline, never to a number from an hour\n   297\t   ago); mark runs `unstable` when the fresh baseline drifts > 10 % from the\n   298\t   pinned one.\n   299\t2. **Model edits the benchmark or the tests.** Rejected by the allowlist (\u00a75.5)."

The prompt allows WASM/NEON paths, but the gate only\n   533\trewards x86.\n   534\t\n   535\t### Divergences from the design above\n   536\t\n   537\t1. **Python, not shell.** `verify.sh` / `bench.sh` are `verify.py` /\n   538\t   `bench.py`: the equality gate needs numpy, and one language keeps the\n   539\t   driver importing the gates directly.\n   540\t2. **A/B oracle is `cedar_bench --dump`, not `cedar_core`.** The dump uses the\n   541\t   exact stimuli the timing path uses (one source of truth), and a baseline and\n   542\t   a candidate binary can run side by side without two `cedar_core` modules\n   543\t   colliding on one import name. `cedar_core` is still built per worktree and\n   544\t   drives the experiment gate.\n   545\t3. **Extra gate: scalar fallback.** Every opcode is also dumped with\n   546\t   `--scalar` (forces `simd::Isa::Scalar`) and must be bit-identical to the\n   547\t   original. This enforces \"the scalar body is never deleted\" mechanically.\n   548\t4. **Noise band = run-to-run spread of baseline medians** over 5 interleaved\n   549\t   rounds. The within-run p10/p90 spread is still reported, but it measures\n   550\t   interrupt tails (30 % on `op_mul`) rather than median noise (0.5 %). The bench\n   551\t   also subtracts an interleaved empty-program VM (~450 ns of fixed\n   552\t   `process_block` overhead that hid most of `op_mul`'s cost) and times each\n   553\t   rep over 32 blocks spread across all four stimulus segments (`tanh`'s cost\n   554\t   depends on the input).\n   555\t5. **No per-ISA kernel TUs.** Kernels carry `[[gnu::target(\"avx2\")]]`\n   556\t   (`CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2`) in the opcode headers, so no CMake flags change and the\n   557\t   allowlist stays header-only. `CEDAR_SIMD` defaults **OFF**; the `simd`\n   558\t   preset (Release + tests) is the gate build, and `wasm-simd` adds\n   559\t   `-msimd128`.\n   560\t6. **Experiment gate = no new `\u2717` lines vs baseline, and exit code 0.** Most\n   561\t   `test_op_*.py` scripts have no assertions and always exit 0, so the\n   562\t   stronger form in \u00a74.3 is not available without editing them.\n   563\t7. **Speed baseline moves, equality baseline does not.** After an accept, later\n   564\t   proposals must beat the accepted head; equality is always against the\n   565\t   original scalar dumps, so approximations cannot compound. Speedup vs origin\n   566\t   is logged too.\n   567\t8. **Edge case 6 not implemented.** C5 is fixed from the Phase 0 ranking, not\n   568\t   nominated by the loop, so \"stop nominating after two losses\" has nothing to\n   569\t   act on."
